def soft_obj_grad(h, beta):
    n=len(h); dx=2/n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full')
    scaled=beta*c; m=scaled.max(); w=np.exp(scaled-m); ws=w.sum(); p=w/ws
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    # iterate over nonzero p
    # positive lags s=0..n-1 index n-1+s
    for s in range(n):
       idx=n-1+s; pw=p[idx]
       if pw==0: continue
       if s==0:
          grad += pw*(1-h) # since lag0: h*(1-h) derivative 1-h
       else:
          grad[s:] += pw*(1-h[:-s])
          grad[:-s] -= pw*h[s:]
    for s in range(1,n):
       idx=n-1-s; pw=p[idx]
       if pw==0: continue
       grad[:n-s] += pw*(1-h[s:])
       grad[s:] -= pw*h[:n-s]
    return obj, grad*dx
```

Projection:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, float).copy()
    target = 0.5*n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff = h.sum() - target
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h > 1e-14
        else:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-14
        if mask.sum() == 0:
            break
        h[mask] -= diff / mask.sum()
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    # final exact distribute among free
    diff = target - h.sum()
    free = (h > 0) & (h < 1)
    if free.sum() > 0 and abs(diff)>1e-15:
        h[free] += diff/free.sum()
        h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # if still off due clipping, adjust boundary? repeat few
    for _ in range(20):

def soft_jac(h, n, beta):
    F=...
    scaled=beta*F; m=scaled.max(); w=np.exp(scaled-m); w /= w.sum()
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    for lag in range(1,2*n-1): ... using w[lag]
    return grad
```
Since F = raw*dx, gradient of F = grad_raw*dx. Our raw gradient formula already can be multiplied by dx inside. We'll compute dx once.
Loop over lags:
```
dx = 2.0/n
grad = zeros(n)
# positive lag s=0..n-1 index n-1+s
p = w[n-1]
if p:
   grad += p*(1.0 - 2.0*h) * dx
for s in range(1,n):
   p = w[n-1+s]
   if p > 0: # >1e-18
      grad[s:] += p*(1.0 - h[:-s])
      grad[:-s] -= p*h[s:]
for s in range(1,n):
   p = w[n-1-s]
   if p > 0:
      grad[:n-s] += p*(1.0 - h[s:])
      grad[s:] -= p*h[:n-s]
return grad*dx
```
Check negative lag index for a=s: `w[n-1-s]` with s=1..n-1. Good.

But if beta high, w underflows for inactive. Use threshold. We can also compute weights using `np.exp(scaled - m)`; if beta F large differences >700, underflow to 0; max weight 1. Fine.

def nlp_obj(x, n):
    return float(x[-1])

def nlp_ineq_fun(x, n, idx):
    h=x[:n]
    F = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')*(2/n)
    return F[idx] - x[-1]

def nlp_ineq_jac(x, n, idx):
    h=x[:n]; m=len(idx)
    J=np.zeros((m,n+1))
    # raw_grad summed over idx
We need gradient of F at selected idx. Use `raw_grad_for_indices(h,n,idx)` but gradient for each F. We can compute by looping idx and adding. For each lag, formula. m up to 80, O(m*n) okay.
    for r, idx_k in enumerate(idx):
       lag=idx_k - (n-1)
       dx=2/n
       if lag>=0:
          if lag==0: row = (1-2h)*dx
          else:
             row[lag:] += (1-h[:n-lag])*dx
             row[:n-lag] -= h[lag:]*dx
       else:
          a=-lag
          row[:n-a] += (1-h[a:])*dx
          row[a:] -= h[:n-a]*dx
       J[r,:n] = row
    J[r,-1] = -1.0

Selecting active:
```
def choose_active(F, C, max_count=60, tol=1e-5):
    order=np.argsort(F)[::-1]
    keep=[]
    for idx in order:
       if F[idx] >= C - max(tol, 1e-6) or len(keep)<10: # include top 10
       if len(keep)>=max_count: break
       keep.append(int(idx))
    return np.array(keep)
```

Greedy function:
```
def binary_improve(h0, n, rng, max_time, t0):
    h = np.round(h0); sort adjust to target.
    best = c5
    zeros = np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
    no_improve=0
    while time < limit and no_improve<some:
       found=False
       # candidate pairs: if pairs many, sample; else all
       if len(zeros)*len(ones)<=2000: sample all with random base
       else: sample 500 random pairs
       best_delta=None
       for i0 in sample zeros, i1 in sample ones:
           h2=h.copy(); h2[i0]=1; h2[i1]=0
           c2=max(...)*dx
           if c2 < best - 1e-12:
              choose candidate with lowest c2 (or first)
       if found: accept; update lists
       else: no_improve++
       if no_improve>10: break
```

Detailed code skeleton:
```
import time, numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def dx_of(n): return 2.0/n

def project_feasible(h, n): ...

def F_values(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx_of(n)
def c5_value(h,n): return float(np.max(F_values(h,n)))

def sum_eq_obj(x,n): return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*n)
def sum_eq_jac(x,n): return np.ones(len(x)) # maybe use n not len if x? For SLSQP with x length n
```
In constraints, `fun` and `jac` called with x and args n. If x includes y? For standard sum only h length n. For NLP separate.

Soft functions:
```
def soft_fun(x, n, beta):
    F = F_values(x,n)
    bF = beta*F
    m = np.max(bF)
    # if m too large? no
    z = np.exp(bF - m)
    return float((m + np.log(np.sum(z))) / beta)

def soft_jac(x, n, beta):
    F=F_values(x,n); bF=beta*F; m=max; w=exp; w/=sum
    n=len(x); dx=2.0/n; grad=np.zeros(n)
    p=w[n-1]
    if p>0: grad += p*(1-2*x)
    for s in range(1,n):
       p = w[n-1+s]
       if p > 1e-18:
          grad[s:] += p*(1.0 - x[:n-s])
          grad[:n-s] -= p*x[s:]
    for s in range(1,n):
       p = w[n-1-s]
       if p > 1e-18:
          grad[:n-s] += p*(1.0 - x[s:])
          grad[s:] -= p*x[:n-s]
    return grad*dx
```
Use `x` not h. Need ensure `x` 1d. SLSQP may pass list; np.asarray.

Hard avg functions:
```
def hard_avg_fun(x,n,tol=1e-5): F=F_values; C=max; active=F >= C - tol; if active.sum()==0: active[0]=1; return C
def hard_avg_jac(x,n,tol=1e-5): ... weights uniform active; call raw_grad_weighted with weights for active indices. Need efficient.
```
Implement `raw_grad_weighted(x, n, idx, weights)` can be used. But for soft, weights over all 2n-1. We can implement a general `apply_lag_weights(x, n, lags_weights)`? Simpler separate.

For hard_jac, active count small. Code:
```
def hard_avg_jac(x,n,tol=1e-5):
    F=F_values(x,n); C=F.max(); active = F >= C - tol
    if not active.any(): active[argmax]=1
    w=active/active.sum()
    return weighted_lag_grad(x,n, np.nonzero(active)[0], w, dx)
```
Define `weighted_lag_grad(x, n, idx, weights, dx)`:
```
def weighted_lag_grad(x,n,idx,weights,dx):
    n=len(x); grad=np.zeros(n)
    center=n-1
    for idx_k, wt in zip(idx, weights):
       if wt <=0: continue
       lag=int(idx_k)-center
       if lag >= 0:
          s=lag
          if s==0: grad += wt*(1-2*x)
          else: grad[s:] += wt*(1-x[:n-s]); grad[:n-s] -= wt*x[s:]
       else:
          s=-lag
          grad[:n-s] += wt*(1-x[s:]); grad[s:] -= wt*x[:n-s]
    return grad*dx
```
Could use for soft too by passing nonzero weights, but soft has many weights; okay.

Soft_jac can use weighted_lag_grad: idx = np.nonzero(w > 1e-18)[0], weights=w[idx]. Then loop. Good. This avoids duplicating.

NLP functions:
```
def nlp_obj(x,n): return float(x[-1])
def nlp_ineq_fun(x,n,idx): h=x[:n]; F=F_values(h,n); return F[idx]-x[-1]
def nlp_ineq_jac(x,n,idx): h=x[:n]; m=len(idx); J=np.zeros((m,n+1)); dx=2/n; for r,k in enumerate(idx): ... row=J[r,:n]; same lag grad raw; J[r,-1]=-1; return J
def nlp_eq_fun(x,n): return float(sum(x[:n])-0.5*n)
def nlp_eq_jac(x,n): J=np.ones(n+1); J[-1]=0; return J
```
No closures.

Binary improve:
```
def binary_greedy(h0, n, rng, t0, limit):
    if n % 2: return h0,best
    target=n//2
    # create binary from h0: choose largest n/2 as 1, with ties? If sum fractional, okay.
    h=np.zeros(n); order=np.argsort(-h0); h[order[:target]]=1
    best=c5_value(h,n)
    zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0); ones=np.flatnonzero(h==1)
    no_imp=0
    while time.time()<limit and no_imp<20:
       listed=False
       if len(zeros)*len(ones) <= 800:
          pairs = [(a,b) for a in zeros for b in ones]
       else:
          if not pairs? Need generate random pairs. Use `pairs=[]` then rng.choice.
       best_pair=None; best_c=best
       sampled = min(300, len(pairs)) if list else 300
       for _ in range(sampled):
          if list: choose index rng.randint
          else: i0=zeros[rng.randrange(len(zeros))]; i1=ones[...]
          if i0==i1 continue
          h2=h.copy(); h2[i0]=1; h2[i1]=0
          c2=c5_value(h2,n)
          if c2 < best_c - 1e-12: best_c=c2; best_pair=(i0,i1)
       if best_pair is not None:
          h[...]=...; best=best_c; update lists; no_imp=0; listed True
       else:
          no_imp +=1
          # optionally perturb: random 5 swaps accept? to escape
          if no_imp > 5:
             # random exchange and maybe continue
    return h,best
```
This greedy only accepts improvements; may stuck. Could add random perturbation: if no improvement for many, apply 2-3 random swaps even if worse but keep best, reset. But binary state not best? We can maintain best_h and use simulated annealing from current. Simpler: add random restarts: binary_greedy from random balanced binary multiple times; keep best.

Upsampling:
```
def interpolate_to(h, n_old, n_new):
    x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*2.0/n_old
    x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*2.0/n_new
    h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return project_feasible(h_new,n_new)
```
For binary to fractional n, if n_new even, binarize.

Local refine function with budget fraction:
```
def refine_local(h0, n, t0, deadline, seed, quick=False):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=c5
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    cons=[...]
    betas = [10,50,200] if quick else [5,20,80,300,1000]
    maxit = [80,120,160,200,300] if quick else ...
    for beta,mi in zip:
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       try:
          res = minimize(soft_fun, h, jac=soft_jac, args=(n,beta), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':mi, 'ftol':1e-12})
          h2=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x),n)
          c2=c5_value(h2,n); if c2<c: update
       except: pass
    # active hard avg if time
    if time.time() < deadline-? :
       for tol in [1e-4,1e-6]: minimize(hard_avg_fun, jac=hard_avg_jac, args=(n,tol), options maxiter 100)
    return h,c
```
Need not overdo SLSQP stages from same h if no improvement. Maybe if beta stage didn't improve, still try.

SLSQP may warn; no issue. Use `np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)`. If `res.x` slightly infeasible, project.

After each refine, check sum. `project_feasible` does. c recompute.

Multi-start details:

   * Smooth low-freq: best + sum a_m cos/sin (random 3 modes), scale 0.3, project.
   * Binarized best (if even, or for n odd we can make h = 0/1 plus one 0.5; but local can handle) If n even.
   * Round + jitter: (h>0.5) + small, project.
- Also low-n upsample: choose n_small in [20,30,40,50], create random h or sinusoid, optimize quick at n_small? Then interpolate to n and refine. Active NLP stage function:
```
def active_nlp_refine(h,n,t0,deadline,maxiter=200, active_tol=1e-4):
   h=project; F=F_values; C=C...
   idx=choose_active(F,C)
   x0=np.concatenate([h,[C]])
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n+[(0,2)]
   cons=[ineq, eq]
   for attempt in range(2):
      if time>deadline break
      try res=minimize(nlp_obj, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
      except: continue
      x=res.x; h2=project(x[:n]); c2=c5(h2); if c2<C: return; update F, idx, x0=...
   return h,c
```

Let's design robust `project_box_sum`.

Euclidean projection onto box and sum. For n up to 200, bisection 60 iterations:
```
def project_box_sum(y, n):
    y=np.asarray(y,float).copy(); S=0.5*n
    if y.sum() < 1e-13: return zeros? but target.
    lo = y.min() - 1.0
    hi = y.max() + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       tau=(lo+hi)/2
       p=np.clip(y - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
       s=p.sum()
       if s > S: lo=tau
       else: hi=tau
    h=np.clip(y - (lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust free
    diff=S-h.sum(); free=(h>0)&(h<1)
    if free.sum()>0:
       h[free]+=diff/free.sum()
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # if still off, iterative adjust from above
    for _ in range(20): ...
    return h
```
Does bisection direction correct? h(tau)=clip(y-tau). As tau increases, h decreases, sum decreases. If sum > S, need larger tau -> lo=tau. If sum <S, need smaller tau -> hi=tau. Initial lo=min(y)-1 makes sum n? If tau very low, clip all 1 maybe sum n; hi=max(y)+1 gives 0. Good.
After free adjust, diff should be 0; clipping can cause small. If h[some] at bounds due adjust? free mean; if diff too large, free adjust may push some out. But projection tau ensures diff small (< machine). Good.
This is O(80*n) vs iterative 100*n; similar. For n=95, okay. Use in all projections. It returns closer exact. For speed, in SLSQP final only. In `snap_feasible` uses. Good.

Let's use `project_box_sum` named `project_feasible`. It accepts n maybe len. If y sum exactly target, tau around mean? Good.

Potential issue: If y contain nan? SLSQP maybe? Project handles? If nan, clip nan? np.clip nan stays. We can `np.nan_to_num`. Add `h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)`. Good.

Now, c5_value for h with n: If `n` not len? Use `n=len(h)` in function to avoid mismatch. But for performance, pass.
```
def c5_value(h, n=None):
    if n is None: n=len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h, mode='full')) * 2.0/n)
```
In helper use n. Ensure n int.

Now, implement `refine_local` with SLSQP. Need to make sure no nested functions for constraints? We define top-level constraint functions. For each n, constraint list referencing them.

```
def refine_local(h0, n, deadline, seed, quick=False):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=c5_value(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_fun,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    if quick:
        schedule=[(20.0,60),(80.0,120)]
    else:
        schedule=[(8.0,120),(30.0,180),(100.0,220),(300.0,260),(1000.0,300)]
    for beta, mi in schedule:
       if time.time() >= deadline - 5: break
       rem = deadline - time.time()
       if rem < 20: break
       maxit = min(mi, 60 if rem<60 else 120 if rem<150 else mi)
       try:
          res = minimize(soft_fun, h, jac=soft_jac, args=(n,beta), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-12})
          h2=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x),n)
          c2=c5_value(h2,n)
          if c2 < c - 1e-12:
             h=h2; c=c2
          # if no improvement maybe still project h and continue
       except Exception:
          pass
       if not quick and c < c0 and time? ...
    # hard avg if not quick or if time
    if (not quick) or (time.time() < deadline - 30):
       for tol, mi in [(1e-4,80),(1e-6,80)]:
          if time.time() >= deadline - 5: break
          try: res=minimize(hard_avg_fun, h, jac=hard_avg_jac, args=(n,tol), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':min(mi, ...), 'ftol':1e-12})
          ... accept if improve
    return h,c
```
Need `hard_avg_fun` signature `(h,n,tol)` and `hard_avg_jac` same. Good.

But SLSQP minimization of hard_avg_fun returns max F (not smoothed) but gradient average active. Since objective is discontinuous in active set, SLSQP may halt. But quick maxiter small.

Maybe use soft beta 3000 instead of hard for final. Add to schedule if full and time: beta 3000 maxiter 100. But beta too high may produce gradient only one active; okay.

Now, `active_nlp_refine` code:
```
def active_nlp_refine(h0, n, deadline, seed, max_counts=(40,80)):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=c5_value(h,n); F=F_values(h,n)
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n + [(0,2)]
    eqcons=[{'type':'eq','fun':nlp_eq_fun,'jac':nlp_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    x0=np.concatenate((h,[c+1e-8]))
    for mc in max_counts:
      if time>deadline: break
      idx=choose_active_idx(F,c,mc,1e-4)
      if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([np.argmax(F)])
      ineq={'type':'ineq','fun':nlp_ineq_fun,'jac':nlp_ineq_jac,'args':(n, idx)}
      cons=[ineq]+eqcons
      try:
         res=minimize(nlp_obj, x0, None, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':200,'ftol':1e-12})
         x2=res.x; h2=project_feasible(x2[:n],n); c2=c5_value(h2,n)
         if c2 < c - 1e-12:
             h=h2; c=c2; F=F_values(h,n); x0=...
      except: pass
    return h,c
```

It will finite difference objective y wrt h? But constraint jacs provided. Objective gradient finite difference for y? It will perturb y and h, many evaluations expensive. def random_smooth_start(n, rng):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    h=0.5*np.ones(n)

State half binary: For n odd, target .5. For even, no half; can use binary with n/2 ones. Implement `make_half_binary` for any n:
```
def make_half_binary(vals,n):
    target=0.5*n
    floor=int(math.floor(target))
    frac=target-floor
    h=np.zeros(n)
    # choose top floor as 1, next one frac, rest 0 based on vals
    order=np.argsort(-vals, kind='stable')
    if floor>0: h[order[:floor]]=1.0
    if frac>1e-12 and floor<n: h[order[floor]]=frac
    # project to correct sum (if floor count? if n even frac=0)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
If target integer, floor=n/2, frac=0. If n=95, floor=47, frac=0.5. Good. But for even n, makes binary. We can use for binary hopping even too.

Pseudo:
```
def binary_hopping(h0,n,rng,deadline,total_random=3):
    best = make_half_binary(h0,n); best_c = c5_value(best,n)
    cur_states = [(best_c,best.copy())]
    for rs in range(total_random):
       if time > deadline: break
       randh = rng.rand(n)
       cand = make_half_binary(randh,n)
       cand_c = c5_value(cand,n)
       if cand_c < best_c: update
       cur = cand.copy(); cur_c=cand_c
       # hillclimb for sub time
       sub_deadline = min(time+20, deadline-10)
       evals=0; no=0
       while (time < sub_deadline or < max_eval_per) and no<400:
          i=rng...; j=...
          h2=cur.copy(); swap
          c2=c5_value(h2,n); evals++
          if c2 < cur_c - 1e-12:
             cur = h2; cur_c=c2; no=0
             if cur_c < best_c - 1e-12: best=cur.copy(); best_c=cur_c
          else: no+=1
    # basin perturbation from best
    swap_sizes=[1,2,3,5]
    restart=0
    while time < deadline - 10:
       size=swap_sizes[restart % len]
       cur=best.copy()
       for _ in range(size): swap random
       cur_c=c5(cur)
       no=0; evals=0
       while no<500 and evals<2000 and time<deadline-10:
          sample swap; c2
          if c2<cur_c: accept update best if improve
          else no++
       restart++
       if restart>50: break
    return best,best_c
```
Need ensure project after swaps? Swapping values from feasible half-binary preserves sum and bounds exactly. Good. If initial make project may produce small epsilon not exact 0/1/0.5? We set 1/0/frac exactly; project may adjust. Swaps preserve.

Add cap `evals` because time enough. Use `max_evals_hill=10000` per basin.

def binary_hopping(h0, n, rng, deadline, total_random=4, max_hill_evals=5000):
    best = make_half_binary(h0, n)
    best_m = max_correlate_only(best)
    # random starts
    for rs in range(total_random):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       seed_vals = rng.rand(n)
       h = make_half_binary(seed_vals, n)
       m = max_correlate_only(h)
       if m < best_m:
           best = h.copy(); best_m=m
       # hillclimb this h
       sub_deadline = min(time.time() + 30.0, deadline - 5.0)
       cur = h.copy(); cur_m=m; no=0; evals=0
       while (time.time() < sub_deadline or evals < 1000) ... Need condition logic. Simpler while time < sub_deadline and evals < max_hill_evals and no < 800.
          if time > deadline: break
          i=rng.randint(0,n); j=rng.randint(0,n-1); if j>=i: j=(j+n-1)%n
          if i==j: continue
          h2=cur.copy(); tmp=cur[i]; cur[i]=cur[j]; cur[j]=tmp? To avoid copy? We need candidate. Use quick:
          cand = cur.copy(); cand[i],cand[j]=cand[j],cand[i]
          m2=max_correlate_only(cand); evals+=1

              cur=cand; cur_m=m2; no=0
              if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12: best=cur.copy(); best_m=cur_m

    # basin hops from best
    sizes=[1,2,3,5]
    hops=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 10 and hops < 60:

       cur=best.copy()

def try_transforms(h, c, n, deadline):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_m = c * n / 2.0
    # snap based on metric
    for eps ...:
       h2=snap_feasible(best_h, eps); m2=max_correlate_only(h2); if m2<best_m: update; c=...
    # reverse
    h2=best_h[::-1].copy(); project? reversal preserves sum but maybe floating; m2...
    # shifts
    if n<=300:
       test=best_h.copy()
       step=1 if n<=150 else 2 if n<=300 else 5
       for s in range(1,n,step):
          h2=np.roll(test,s); m2=max_correlate_only(h2); if m2<best_m: update best_h
       if time<deadline: rev=best_h[::-1].copy(); for s in range(0,n,step): ...

```

import time

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    y = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    if n == 0:
        return y
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        p = np.clip(y - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = tau
        else:
            hi = tau
    h = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    free = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
    if free.sum() > 0 and abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        h[free] += diff / float(free.sum())
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
        cnt = int(mask.sum())
        if cnt == 0:
            break
        h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
For diff negative, adding negative to low values (h>1e-12) decreases them; if h all zeros? target .5n no. If diff positive and all ones? no. Good.

`max_correlate_only`:
```
def max_correlate_only(h):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")))
```
`c5_value`:
```
def c5_value(h, n):
    return max_correlate_only(h) * (2.0 / float(n))
```
`F_values`:
```
def F_values(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / float(n))
```

Sum constraints:
```
def sum_eq_fun(x, n): return float(np.sum(np.asarray(x)) - 0.5*float(n))
def sum_eq_jac(x, n): return np.ones(int(n), dtype=float)
```
If x length maybe n. Good.

`weighted_lag_grad`:
```
def weighted_lag_grad(x,n,lags,weights,dx):
    n=int(n); x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    grad=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    center=n-1
    for lag,val in zip(lags, weights):
        w=float(val)
        if w <= 1e-18: continue
        lag=int(lag); s=lag-center
        if s >= 0:
            if s == 0:
                grad += w * (1.0 - 2.0*x)
            else:
                grad[s:] += w * (1.0 - x[:n-s])
                grad[:n-s] -= w * x[s:]
        else:
            a=-s
            grad[:n-a] += w * (1.0 - x[a:])
            grad[a:] -= w * x[:n-a]
    return grad * dx
```
For s positive, if s=n? max n-1. Good.

Soft:
```
def soft_fun(x,n,beta):
    F=F_values(x,n)
    scaled=beta*F
    m=float(np.max(scaled))
    z=np.exp(scaled-m)
    return float((m + np.log(np.sum(z))) / beta)
```
If beta small? >0.

`soft_jac`:
```
def soft_jac(x,n,beta):
    F=F_values(x,n)
    scaled=beta*F
    m=float(np.max(scaled))
    w=np.exp(scaled-m)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.zeros(len(x))
    w=w/s
    n=int(n); mask=w>1e-15
    lags=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    weights=w[lags]
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    return weighted_lag_grad(x,n,lags,weights,dx)
```

Hard:
```
def hard_avg_fun(x,n,tol):
    F=F_values(x,n); C=float(np.max(F)); return C
def hard_avg_jac(x,n,tol):
    F=F_values(x,n); C=float(np.max(F))
    active = F >= C - float(tol)
    if not active.any():
        active = np.zeros_like(F, dtype=bool); active[int(np.argmax(F))]=True
    idx=np.nonzero(active)[0]
    w=np.ones(idx.size)/float(idx.size)
    n=int(n); dx=2.0/float(n)
    return weighted_lag_grad(x,n,idx,w,dx)
```

NLP:
```
def nlp_obj(x,n): return float(np.asarray(x)[-1])
def nlp_obj_jac(x,n):
    x=np.asarray(x); grad=np.zeros(x.size); grad[-1]=1.0; return grad
def nlp_ineq_fun(x,n,idx):
    x=np.asarray(x); h=x[:n]
    F=F_values(h,n)
    return F[np.asarray(idx,dtype=int)] - x[-1]
def nlp_ineq_jac(x,n,idx):
    x=np.asarray(x); n=int(n); h=x[:n]
    idx=np.asarray(idx,dtype=int)
    m=idx.size
    J=np.zeros((m,n+1), dtype=float)
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    center=n-1
    for r,k in enumerate(idx):
       lag=int(k)-center; s=lag
       row=J[r,:n]
       if s>=0:
          if s==0: row[:] = 1.0 - 2.0*h
          else:
             row[s:] += 1.0 - h[:n-s]
             row[:n-s] -= h[s:]
       else:
          a=-s
          row[:n-a] += 1.0 - h[a:]
          row[a:] -= h[:n-a]
       row *= dx
       J[r,-1] = -1.0
    return J
def nlp_eq_fun(x,n): return float(np.sum(np.asarray(x)[:int(n)]) - 0.5*float(n))
def nlp_eq_jac(x,n):
    n=int(n); grad=np.zeros(n+1); grad[:n]=1.0; return grad
```
For s>=0 row may have uninitialized? row zeros. Good.

`choose_active_idx`:
```
def choose_active_idx(F,C,max_count=40,tol=1e-4):
    F=np.asarray(F)
    C=float(C)
    top=np.argsort(F)[::-1]
    idx=[]; seen=set()
    tol=max(float(tol),1e-8)
    for i in top:
       i=int(i)
       if F[i] >= C - tol and i not in seen:
          idx.append(i); seen.add(i)
       if len(idx) >= max_count: break
    if len(idx) == 0:
       idx.append(int(top[0])); seen.add(int(top[0]))
    need = min(12, max_count)
    if len(idx) < need:
       for i in top:
          i=int(i)
          if i not in seen:
             idx.append(i); seen.add(i)
          if len(idx) >= need: break
    if len(idx) > max_count:
       idx=idx[:max_count]
    return np.array(idx, dtype=int)
```
This includes top 12 even if far. Good.

`active_nlp_refine`:
```
def active_nlp_refine(h0,n,deadline):
    n=int(n); h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=c5_value(h,n)
    F=F_values(h,n); C=float(np.max(F))
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    bounds.append((0.0,2.0))
    eq_cons={'type':'eq','fun':nlp_eq_fun,'jac':nlp_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}
    x0=np.empty(n+1); x0[:n]=h; x0[-1]=C+1e-8
    for mc in (35,70):
        if time.time() >= deadline: break
        idx=choose_active_idx(F,C,mc,1e-4)
        if idx.size == 0: continue
        ineq={'type':'ineq','fun':nlp_ineq_fun,'jac':nlp_ineq_jac,'args':(n,idx)}
        cons=[ineq, eq_cons]
        try:
            res=minimize(nlp_obj,x0,jac=nlp_obj_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,
                         options={'maxiter':180,'ftol':1e-12})
            x2=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
            h2=project_feasible(x2[:n],n)
            c2=c5_value(h2,n)
            if c2 < c - 1e-12:
                h=h2; c=c2; F=F_values(h,n); C=float(np.max(F)); x0=np.empty(n+1); x0[:n]=h; x0[-1]=C+1e-8
        except Exception:
            pass
    return h,c
```
Potential: SLSQP may call objective/jac with x not shape n+1? OK.

`refine_local`: Need define options based on rem. Could be:
```
def refine_local(h0,n,deadline,seed,quick=False):
    n=int(n); h=project_feasible(h0,n); c=c5_value(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_fun,'jac':sum_eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    if quick:
       schedule=[(20.0,50),(80.0,100),(300.0,150)]
    else:
       schedule=[(5.0,80),(20.0,140),(80.0,180),(300.0,240),(1000.0,280)]
    improved_total=False
    for beta,mi in schedule:
       now=time.time()
       if now >= deadline - 5: break
       rem=deadline-now
       if rem < 15: break
       if rem < 60: maxit=min(mi,40)
       elif rem < 150: maxit=min(mi,100)
       else: maxit=mi
       try:
          res=minimize(soft_fun,h,jac=soft_jac,args=(n,beta),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':int(maxit),'ftol':1e-11})
          h2=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float),n)
          c2=c5_value(h2,n)
          if c2 < c - 1e-12:
             h=h2; c=c2
       except Exception:
          pass
    # hard avg
    now=time.time()
    if now < deadline - 20:
       for tol in (1e-4,1e-6):
          if time.time() >= deadline - 5: break
          rem=deadline-time.time()
          maxit=40 if rem<60 else 80
          try:
             res=minimize(hard_avg_fun,h,jac=hard_avg_jac,args=(n,tol),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':int(maxit),'ftol':1e-11})
             h2=project_feasible(...); c2=...
             if c2 < c - 1e-12: update
          except: pass
    return h,c
```
For quick, maybe skip hard if rem small. Good.

Potential: SLSQP with disabled? If `res.status` negative, res.x may be x0. OK.

`make_half_binary`:
```
def make_half_binary(vals,n):
    n=int(n)
    y=np.asarray(vals,dtype=float).ravel()
    target=0.5*float(n)
    floor=int(np.floor(target))
    frac=target - float(floor)
    h=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    if floor > 0:
       order=np.argsort(-y, kind='stable')
       h[order[:floor]]=1.0
    if frac > 1e-10:
       order=np.argsort(-y, kind='stable')
       if floor < n:
          h[order[floor]]=frac
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
If n=0? no. It sorts twice; could sort once. Let's optimize:
```
order=np.argsort(-y, kind='stable')
h[order[:floor]]=1.0
if frac>... and floor<n: h[order[floor]]=frac
```
If floor=0? for n<2, not. Good.

`binary_hopping`:
Top-level. Need ensure rng is RandomState. Code:
```
def binary_hopping(h0,n,rng,deadline,total_random=4,max_hill_evals=5000):
    n=int(n)
    best=make_half_binary(h0,n)
    best_m=max_correlate_only(best)
    # helper local variables not functions
    # random starts
    for rs in range(max(0,int(total_random))):
       now=time.time()
       if now >= deadline - 10: break
       vals=rng.rand(n)
       cur=make_half_binary(vals,n)
       cur_m=max_correlate_only(cur)
       if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
          best=cur.copy(); best_m=cur_m
       # hillclimb only if close
       if cur_m <= best_m + max(2.0, 0.25*best_m) or rs == 0:
          sub_deadline = min(time.time() + 25.0, deadline - 10.0)
          no=0; evals=0
          while (time.time() < sub_deadline) and (evals < max_hill_evals) and (no < 600):
             if time.time() >= deadline: break
             i=int(rng.randint(0,n)); j=int(rng.randint(0,n-1))
             if j >= i: j = (j + n - 1) % n
             if i == j: continue
             cand=cur.copy(); cand[i],cand[j]=cand[j],cand[i]
             cand_m=max_correlate_only(cand)
             evals += 1
             if cand_m < cur_m - 1e-12:
                cur=cand; cur_m=cand_m; no=0
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                    best=cur.copy(); best_m=cur_m
             else:
                no += 1
    # basin hops
    sizes=[1,2,3,5]
    hops=0; no_improve=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 10 and hops < 80 and no_improve < 12:
       size=sizes[hops % len(sizes)]
       cur=best.copy()
       for _ in range(size):
          i=int(rng.randint(0,n)); j=int(rng.randint(0,n-1))
          if j >= i: j = (j+n-1) % n
          if i != j:
             tmp=cur[i]; cur[i]=cur[j]; cur[j]=tmp
       cur_m=max_correlate_only(cur)
       sub_deadline = min(time.time() + 35.0, deadline - 10.0)
       no=0; evals=0; improved_here=False
       while (time.time() < sub_deadline) and (evals < max_hill_evals) and (no < 800):
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          i=...; j=...
          if i==j: continue
          cand=cur.copy(); swap
          cand_m=max_correlate_only(cand); evals+=1
          if cand_m < cur_m - 1e-12:
             cur=cand; cur_m=cand_m; no=0
             if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                best=cur.copy(); best_m=cur_m; improved_here=True
          else: no +=1
       hops +=1
       if not improved_here:
          no_improve +=1
       else:
          no_improve =0
    return best, float(best_m)
```
If n=1, j logic? n likely >2. Could guard if n<2 return. Add:
```
if n < 2: return h0, max_correlate_only(h0)
```
For random pair, `rng.randint(0,n-1)` if n-1=0? guard.

This binary_hopping can spend time on each random start. If deadline large (400s), total_random 4*25s=100s; basin maybe 7 hops*35=245s plus evals cap. OK.

`interpolate_to`:
```
def interpolate_to(h,n_old,n_new):
    n_old=int(n_old); n_new=int(n_new); h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    if n_old == n_new:
       return project_feasible(h,n_new)
    x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*(2.0/float(n_old))
    x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*(2.0/float(n_new))
    out=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return project_feasible(out,n_new)
```

`snap_feasible`:
```
def snap_feasible(h,eps):
    h2=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h2[h2 < eps] = 0.0
    h2[h2 > 1.0 - eps] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h2, len(h2))
```

`try_transforms`: Use metric.
```
def try_transforms(h,c,n,deadline):
    n=int(n); best_h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(); best_m=float(c)*float(n)/2.0
    # snap
    for eps in (1e-7,1e-5,1e-4):
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       h2=snap_feasible(best_h,eps)
       m2=max_correlate_only(h2)
       if m2 < best_m - 1e-12:
          best_h=h2; best_m=m2
    # reverse
    if time.time() < deadline:
       h2=best_h[::-1].copy()
       m2=max_correlate_only(h2)
       if m2 < best_m -1e-12:
          best_h=h2; best_m=m2
    # shifts if n <= 350
    if n <= 350 and time.time() < deadline - 2:
       step = 1 if n <= 150 else 2 if n <= 350 else 5
       # forward shifts of best_h
       for s in range(1,n,step):
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          h2=np.roll(best_h,s)
          m2=max_correlate_only(h2)
          if m2 < best_m - 1e-12:
             best_h=h2; best_m=m2
    # reverse shifts if n smaller
    if n <= 300 and time.time() < deadline - 2:
       rev=best_h[::-1].copy()
       for s in range(0,n,step):
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          h2=np.roll(rev,s)
          m2=max_correlate_only(h2)
          if m2 < best_m - 1e-12:
             best_h=h2; best_m=m2
    return best_h, float(best_m)*2.0/float(n)
```
For n=95, shifts 94 evals. Good.
Note if best_h updated in forward shifts, we still roll original? We set best_h to shifted but continue `np.roll(best_h,s)` would be different (shift of shifted). Actually we want all shifts relative to original or current? If best_h changes, rolling current by s gives shift s + previous shift, not exhaustive maybe. But checking all s relative to original is enough. If we update best_h inside loop, subsequent `np.roll(best_h,s)` uses new best, causing non-monotonic but still valid; could miss original later shifts? Suppose at s=10 update; then at s=11 do roll(current,11) = original shift21, not original shift11. We should store `base=best_h.copy()` before loop and roll base by s. If update, update best but base remains. Do that.
```
base=best_h.copy()
for s ... h2=np.roll(base,s)
```
After loop, base maybe not updated for reverse; use current best_h as base for reverse shifts? Any shift of reversed best is valid; use current best. Good.

`smooth_perturb`:
```
def smooth_perturb(base,n,rng,scale):
    n=int(n); base=np.asarray(base,dtype=float)
    noise=rng.randn(n)
    if n >= 5:
       k=np.ones(5)/5.0
       noise=np.convolve(noise,k,mode='same')
    else:
       noise = np.mean(noise)
       noise[0]=...
    h=base + float(scale)*noise
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
For n<5, simple. But n always large.
```
def random_smooth_start(n,rng):
    n=int(n)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/float(n))
    h=0.5*np.ones(n)
    num=int(rng.randint(2,5))
    for _ in range(num):
       m=int(rng.randint(1,6))
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.15,0.45))
       phi=float(rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi))
       h += amp*np.cos(np.pi*m*x + phi)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
`make_limit`:
```
def make_limit(t_now, stop, max_seconds, reserve):
    t_now=float(t_now); stop=float(stop); max_seconds=float(max_seconds); reserve=float(reserve)
    if t_now >= stop - reserve:
       return t_now
    return min(t_now + max_seconds, stop - reserve)
```
If stop-reserve < t_now + max, maybe less than t_now? But we checked t_now < stop-reserve, so stop-reserve > t_now. Good.

Function `force_exact_sum(h,n)` top-level:
```
def force_exact_sum(h,n):
    target=0.5*float(n)
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    for _ in range(20):
        s=float(np.sum(h))
        diff=target-s
        if diff == 0.0: break
        free=np.flatnonzero((h>0.0)&(h<1.0))
        if free.size ==0: break
        # pick free where h+diff in [0,1]
        idx=None
        for f in free:
            v=h[f]+diff
            if 0.0 <= v <= 1.0:
                idx=f; break
        if idx is None:
            h[free] += diff/float(free.size)
        else:
            h[idx] += diff
        h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
Add and call at end after project. But if project already close, this okay. Need no closure.

Now, potential issue: In `soft_fun`, beta can be 5 and F values length 2n-1. logsumexp returns soft sum approx maybe not max? Wait soft max formula: (m + logsum_offset)/beta where scaled=beta F. If beta>0. This yields logsumexp(beta F)/beta. If beta small, soft sum large? For beta=5, F~0.38, betaF~1.9, logsum of 189 terms ~ log(189* e^1.9) ~13.4, /5=2.68, much larger than max 0.38. Oops!        if update...

       if n <= 120:

          if c2 < best_c -1e-12: best_h,best_c=h2,c2
       lim2=make_limit(time.time(), stop, 25, 10)
       if lim2 > time.time()+5:
          h2,c2=refine_local(best_h,n,lim2,seed+31,quick=True)
          if c2 < best_c -1e-12: best_h,best_c=h2,c2
    # final transforms/snap
    lim=make_limit(time.time(), stop, 20, 5)
    if lim > time.time(): best_h,best_c=try_transforms(best_h,best_c,n,lim)
    # exact sum and return
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_h = force_exact_sum(best_h,n)
    best_c = c5_value(best_h,n)
    # one more snap
    for eps in (1e-7,1e-5):
        h2=snap_feasible(best_h,eps)
        c2=c5_value(h2,n)
        if c2 < best_c -1e-12:
            best_h=h2; best_c=c2
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_h=force_exact_sum(best_h,n)
    best_c=c5_value(best_h,n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(n)

def force_exact_sum(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(); target=0.5*float(n)
    for _ in range(30):
       s=float(np.sum(h)); diff=target-s
       if diff == 0.0: break
       free=np.flatnonzero((h>0.0)&(h<1.0))
       if free.size ==0:
          # if all at bounds, fallback tiny distribute
          free=np.flatnonzero((h>=0.0)&(h<=1.0))
          if free.size==0: break
          if diff>0: free=free[np.argsort(h[free])]??
          h[free] += diff/float(free.size)
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
          continue
       idx=None

           if 0.0 <= v <= 1.0:
               idx=int(f); break
       if idx is None:
           h[free] += diff/float(free.size)
       else:
           h[idx] += diff
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def project_feasible(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    y = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    if n == 0:
        return y

    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        p = np.clip(y - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = tau
        else:
            hi = tau

    h = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    free = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
    if free.sum() > 0 and abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        h[free] += diff / float(free.sum())
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
        cnt = int(mask.sum())
        if cnt == 0:
            break
        h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    return h


def force_exact_sum(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(30):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if diff == 0.0:
            break

        free = np.flatnonzero((h > 0.0) & (h < 1.0))
        if free.size == 0:
            all_free = np.flatnonzero((h >= 0.0) & (h <= 1.0))
            if all_free.size == 0:
                break
            h[all_free] += diff / float(all_free.size)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
            continue

        idx = None
        for f in free:
            v = float(h[f]) + diff
            if 0.0 <= v <= 1.0:
                idx = int(f)
                break

        if idx is None:
            h[free] += diff / float(free.size)
        else:
            h[idx] += diff
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    return h


def max_correlate_only(h):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")))


def c5_value(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    return max_correlate_only(h) * (2.0 / float(n))


def F_values(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / float(n))


def sum_eq_fun(x, n):
    n = int(n)
    return float(np.sum(np.asarray(x, dtype=float)) - 0.5 * float(n))


def sum_eq_jac(x, n):
    n = int(n)
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def weighted_lag_grad(x, n, lags, weights, dx):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1

    for lag, val in zip(lags, weights):
        w = float(val)
        if w <= 1e-18:
            continue
        lag = int(lag)
        s = lag - center
        if s >= 0:
            if s == 0:
                grad += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
            else:
                grad[s:] += w * (1.0 - x[:n - s])
                grad[:n - s] -= w * x[s:]
        else:
            a = -s
            grad[:n - a] += w * (1.0 - x[a:])
            grad[a:] -= w * x[:n - a]

    return grad * dx


def soft_fun(x, n, beta):
    n = int(n)
    F = F_values(x, n)
    beta = float(beta)
    scaled = beta * F
    m = float(np.max(scaled))
    z = np.exp(scaled - m)
    return float((m + np.log(np.sum(z))) / beta)


def soft_jac(x, n, beta):
    n = int(n)
    F = F_values(x, n)
    beta = float(beta)
    scaled = beta * F
    m = float(np.max(scaled))
    w = np.exp(scaled - m)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w = w / s
    mask = w > 1e-15
    lags = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    weights = w[lags]
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return weighted_lag_grad(x, n, lags, weights, dx)


def hard_avg_fun(x, n, tol):
    n = int(n)
    F = F_values(x, n)
    return float(np.max(F))


def hard_avg_jac(x, n, tol):
    n = int(n)
    F = F_values(x, n)
    C = float(np.max(F))
    active = F >= (C - float(tol))
    if not active.any():
        active = np.zeros(F.shape, dtype=bool)
        active[int(np.argmax(F))] = True
    idx = np.nonzero(active)[0]
    w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=float) / float(idx.size)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return weighted_lag_grad(x, n, idx, w, dx)


def nlp_obj(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    return float(x[-1])


def nlp_obj_jac(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    grad = np.zeros(x.size, dtype=float)
    grad[-1] = 1.0
    return grad


def nlp_ineq_fun(x, n, idx):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    h = x[:n]
    F = F_values(h, n)
    return F[np.asarray(idx, dtype=int)] - x[-1]


def nlp_ineq_jac(x, n, idx):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    h = x[:n]
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=int)
    m = int(idx.size)
    J = np.zeros((m, n + 1), dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    center = n - 1

    for r, k in enumerate(idx):
        lag = int(k) - center
        row = J[r, :n]
        if lag >= 0:
            if lag == 0:
                row[:] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
            else:
                row[lag:] += 1.0 - h[:n - lag]
                row[:n - lag] -= h[lag:]
        else:
            a = -lag
            row[:n - a] += 1.0 - h[a:]
            row[a:] -= h[:n - a]
        row *= dx
        J[r, -1] = -1.0

    return J


def nlp_eq_fun(x, n):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    return float(np.sum(x[:n]) - 0.5 * float(n))


def nlp_eq_jac(x, n):
    n = int(n)
    grad = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    grad[:n] = 1.0
    return grad


def choose_active_idx(F, C, max_count=40, tol=1e-4):
    F = np.asarray(F, dtype=float)
    C = float(C)
    top = np.argsort(F)[::-1]
    max_count = int(max_count)
    tol = max(float(tol), 1e-8)

    idx = []
    seen = set()

    for i in top:
        i = int(i)
        if (F[i] >= C - tol) and (i not in seen):
            idx.append(i)
            seen.add(i)
        if len(idx) >= max_count:
            break

    if not idx:
        i = int(top[0])
        idx.append(i)
        seen.add(i)

    need = min(12, max_count)
    if len(idx) < need:
        for i in top:
            i = int(i)
            if i not in seen:
                idx.append(i)
                seen.add(i)
            if len(idx) >= need:
                break

    if len(idx) > max_count:
        idx = idx[:max_count]

    return np.array(idx, dtype=int)


def active_nlp_refine(h0, n, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c = c5_value(h, n)
    F = F_values(h, n)
    C = float(np.max(F))

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    bounds.append((0.0, 2.0))

    eq_cons = {"type": "eq", "fun": nlp_eq_fun, "jac": nlp_eq_jac, "args": (n,)}

    x0 = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    x0[:n] = h
    x0[-1] = C + 1e-8

    for mc in (35, 70):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break

        idx = choose_active_idx(F, C, mc, 1e-4)
        if idx.size == 0:
            continue

        ineq_cons = {"type": "ineq", "fun": nlp_ineq_fun, "jac": nlp_ineq_jac, "args": (n, idx)}
        cons = [ineq_cons, eq_cons]

        try:
            res = minimize(
                nlp_obj,
                x0,
                jac=nlp_obj_jac,
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": 180, "ftol": 1e-12},
            )
            x2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            h2 = project_feasible(x2[:n], n)
            c2 = c5_value(h2, n)
            if c2 < c - 1e-12:
                h = h2
                c = c2
                F = F_values(h, n)
                C = float(np.max(F))
                x0 = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
                x0[:n] = h
                x0[-1] = C + 1e-8
        except Exception:
            pass

    return h, float(c)


def refine_local(h0, n, deadline, seed, quick=False):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c = c5_value(h, n)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_eq_fun, "jac": sum_eq_jac, "args": (n,)}]

    if quick:
        schedule = [
            (30.0, 60),
            (150.0, 120),
            (600.0, 160),
        ]
    else:
        schedule = [
            (10.0, 90),
            (50.0, 150),
            (200.0, 220),
            (800.0, 260),
            (2500.0, 200),
        ]

    for beta, mi in schedule:
        now = time.time()
        if now >= deadline - 5.0:
            break
        rem = float(deadline) - now
        if rem < 15.0:
            break

        if rem < 60.0:
            maxit = min(int(mi), 40)
        elif rem < 150.0:
            maxit = min(int(mi), 100)
        else:
            maxit = int(mi)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                soft_fun,
                h,
                jac=soft_jac,
                args=(n, beta),
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": maxit, "ftol": 1e-11},
            )
            h2 = project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
            c2 = c5_value(h2, n)
            if c2 < c - 1e-12:
                h = h2
                c = c2
        except Exception:
            pass

    now = time.time()
    if now < deadline - 20.0:
        for tol in (1e-4, 1e-6):
            now = time.time()
            if now >= deadline - 5.0:
                break
            rem = float(deadline) - now
            maxit = 40 if rem < 60.0 else 80
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    hard_avg_fun,
                    h,
                    jac=hard_avg_jac,
                    args=(n, tol),
                    method="SLSQP",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    constraints=cons,
                    options={"maxiter": maxit, "ftol": 1e-11},
                )
                h2 = project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
                c2 = c5_value(h2, n)
                if c2 < c - 1e-12:
                    h = h2
                    c = c2
            except Exception:
                pass

    return h, float(c)


def make_half_binary(vals, n):
    n = int(n)
    y = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float).ravel()
    if y.size != n:
        y = np.resize(y, n)

    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    floor = int(np.floor(target))
    frac = target - float(floor)

    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(-y, kind="stable")

    if floor > 0:
        h[order[:floor]] = 1.0

    if frac > 1e-10 and floor < n:
        h[order[floor]] = frac

    return project_feasible(h, n)


def binary_hopping(h0, n, rng, deadline, total_random=4, max_hill_evals=5000):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
        return h0, float(max_correlate_only(h0))

    best = make_half_binary(h0, n)
    best_m = float(max_correlate_only(best))

    total_random = max(0, int(total_random))
    max_hill_evals = max(100, int(max_hill_evals))

    for rs in range(total_random):
        now = time.time()
        if now >= float(deadline) - 10.0:
            break

        vals = rng.rand(n)
        cur = make_half_binary(vals, n)
        cur_m = float(max_correlate_only(cur))

        if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_m = cur_m

        close_enough = (cur_m <= best_m + max(2.0, 0.25 * best_m)) or (rs == 0)
        if not close_enough:
            continue

        sub_deadline = min(time.time() + 25.0, float(deadline) - 10.0)
        no = 0
        evals = 0

        while (time.time() < sub_deadline) and (evals < max_hill_evals) and (no < 600):
            if time.time() >= float(deadline):
                break

            i = int(rng.randint(0, n))
            j = int(rng.randint(0, n - 1))
            if j >= i:
                j = (j + n - 1) % n
            if i == j:
                continue

            cand = cur.copy()
            cand[i], cand[j] = cand[j], cand[i]
            cand_m = float(max_correlate_only(cand))
            evals += 1

            if cand_m < cur_m - 1e-12:
                cur = cand
                cur_m = cand_m
                no = 0
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                    best = cur.copy()
                    best_m = cur_m
            else:
                no += 1

    sizes = [1, 2, 3, 5]
    hops = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while (time.time() < float(deadline) - 10.0) and (hops < 80) and (no_improve < 12):
        size = sizes[hops % len(sizes)]
        cur = best.copy()

        for _ in range(size):
            i = int(rng.randint(0, n))
            j = int(rng.randint(0, n - 1))
            if j >= i:
                j = (j + n - 1) % n
            if i != j:
                tmp = cur[i]
                cur[i] = cur[j]
                cur[j] = tmp

        cur_m = float(max_correlate_only(cur))
        sub_deadline = min(time.time() + 35.0, float(deadline) - 10.0)
        no = 0
        evals = 0
        improved_here = False

        while (time.time() < sub_deadline) and (evals < max_hill_evals) and (no < 800):
            if time.time() >= float(deadline):
                break

            i = int(rng.randint(0, n))
            j = int(rng.randint(0, n - 1))
            if j >= i:
                j = (j + n - 1) % n
            if i == j:
                continue

            cand = cur.copy()
            cand[i], cand[j] = cand[j], cand[i]
            cand_m = float(max_correlate_only(cand))
            evals += 1

            if cand_m < cur_m - 1e-12:
                cur = cand
                cur_m = cand_m
                no = 0
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-12:
                    best = cur.copy()
                    best_m = cur_m
                    improved_here = True
            else:
                no += 1

        hops += 1
        if improved_here:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

    return best, float(best_m)


def interpolate_to(h, n_old, n_new):
    n_old = int(n_old)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_feasible(h, n_new)
    if n_old <= 0 or n_new <= 0:
        return project_feasible(h, n_new)

    x_old = (np.arange(n_old) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_old))
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_new))
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return project_feasible(out, n_new)


def snap_feasible(h, eps):
    h2 = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h2[h2 < eps] = 0.0
    h2[h2 > 1.0 - eps] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h2, len(h2))


def try_transforms(h, c, n, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    best_h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    best_m = float(c) * float(n) / 2.0

    for eps in (1e-7, 1e-5, 1e-4):
        if time.time() >= float(deadline):
            break
        h2 = snap_feasible(best_h, eps)
        m2 = float(max_correlate_only(h2))
        if m2 < best_m - 1e-12:
            best_h = h2
            best_m = m2

    if time.time() < float(deadline):
        h2 = best_h[::-1].copy()
        m2 = float(max_correlate_only(h2))
        if m2 < best_m - 1e-12:
            best_h = h2
            best_m = m2

    if n <= 350 and time.time() < float(deadline) - 2.0:
        step = 1 if n <= 150 else 2
        base = best_h.copy()
        for s in range(1, n, step):
            if time.time() >= float(deadline):
                break
            h2 = np.roll(base, s)
            m2 = float(max_correlate_only(h2))
            if m2 < best_m - 1e-12:
                best_h = h2
                best_m = m2
        base = best_h.copy()

    if n <= 300 and time.time() < float(deadline) - 2.0:
        rev = best_h[::-1].copy()
        step = 1 if n <= 150 else 2
        for s in range(0, n, step):
            if time.time() >= float(deadline):
                break
            h2 = np.roll(rev, s)
            m2 = float(max_correlate_only(h2))
            if m2 < best_m - 1e-12:
                best_h = h2
                best_m = m2

    return best_h, float(best_m) * (2.0 / float(n))


def smooth_perturb(base, n, rng, scale):
    n = int(n)
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=float)
    noise = rng.randn(n)
    if n >= 5:
        k = np.ones(5, dtype=float) / 5.0
        noise = np.convolve(noise, k, mode="same")
    else:
        noise = np.full(n, float(np.mean(noise)), dtype=float)
    h = base + float(scale) * noise
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def random_smooth_start(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    num = int(rng.randint(2, 5))
    for _ in range(num):
        m = int(rng.randint(1, 6))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.45))
        phi = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
        h += amp * np.cos(np.pi * m * x + phi)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_limit(t_now, stop, max_seconds, reserve):
    t_now = float(t_now)
    stop = float(stop)
    max_seconds = float(max_seconds)
    reserve = float(reserve)
    if t_now >= stop - reserve:
        return t_now
    return min(t_now + max_seconds, stop - reserve)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    stop = t0 + float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    try:
        h_init = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(len(h_init))
    except Exception:
        n = 95
        h_init = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    if n < 2:
        n = 95
        h_init = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    best_h = project_feasible(h_init, n)
    best_c = c5_value(best_h, n)

    lim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 25, 80)
    if lim > time.time():
        best_h, best_c = try_transforms(best_h, best_c, n, lim)

    lim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 260, 450)
    if (lim > time.time() + 15.0) and (n <= 250):
        h2, c2 = refine_local(best_h, n, lim, seed, quick=False)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
        lim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 10, 400)
        if lim > time.time():
            best_h, best_c = try_transforms(best_h, best_c, n, lim)

    lim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 120, 320)
    if (lim > time.time() + 10.0) and (n <= 160):
        h2, c2 = active_nlp_refine(best_h, n, lim)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
        lim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 5, 300)
        if lim > time.time():
            best_h, best_c = try_transforms(best_h, best_c, n, lim)

    if (time.time() < stop - 260.0) and (n <= 160):
        binary_deadline = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 480, 260)

        cand_ns = []
        if n <= 160:
            cand_ns.append(n)
        for nn in (n + 1 if (n % 2) else n, 96, 100, 120):
            if (nn % 2 == 0) and (2 <= nn <= 220) and (nn not in cand_ns):
                cand_ns.append(nn)
        cand_ns = cand_ns[:3]

        for nn in cand_ns:
            now = time.time()
            if now >= binary_deadline - 30.0:
                break
            nn = int(nn)
            if nn < n:
                continue

            if nn == n:
                h_start = best_h.copy()
                total_random = 2
            else:
                h_start = interpolate_to(best_h, n, nn)
                total_random = 4

            max_evals = 3500 if nn <= 120 else 2500
            sub_dead = make_limit(now, binary_deadline, 240, 30)
            if sub_dead <= now + 20.0:
                continue

            h_b, m_b = binary_hopping(
                h_start,
                nn,
                rng,
                sub_dead,
                total_random=total_random,
                max_hill_evals=max_evals,
            )
            c_b = float(m_b) * (2.0 / float(nn))

            if c_b < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h = h_b.copy()
                n = nn
                best_c = c_b

                lim = make_limit(time.time(), binary_deadline, 20, 20)
                if lim > time.time():
                    best_h, best_c = try_transforms(best_h, best_c, n, lim)

                if time.time() < stop - 200.0:
                    reflim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 180, 200)
                    if reflim > time.time() + 15.0:
                        h_r, c_r = refine_local(best_h, n, reflim, seed + 11, quick=True)
                        lim2 = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 5, 150)
                        if lim2 > time.time():
                            h_r, c_r = try_transforms(h_r, c_r, n, lim2)
                        if c_r < best_c - 1e-12:
                            best_h, best_c = h_r, c_r

    start_stop = stop - 120.0
    if (time.time() < start_stop) and (n <= 170):
        modes = [
            ("half", 0.0),
            ("pert", 0.03),
            ("pert", 0.06),
            ("pert", 0.10),
            ("rand", 0.0),
            ("rhalf", 0.0),
        ]
        attempts = 0

        while (time.time() < start_stop) and (attempts < 35):
            mode, scale = modes[attempts % len(modes)]
            attempts += 1

            if mode == "half":
                cand = make_half_binary(best_h, n)
            elif mode == "pert":
                cand = smooth_perturb(best_h, n, rng, scale)
            elif mode == "rand":
                cand = random_smooth_start(n, rng)
            elif mode == "rhalf":
                cand = make_half_binary(random_smooth_start(n, rng), n)
            else:
                cand = random_smooth_start(n, rng)

            cc = c5_value(cand, n)
            if cc <= best_c + 0.06:
                q_lim = make_limit(time.time(), start_stop, 45, 70)
                if q_lim > time.time() + 10.0:
                    h2, c2 = refine_local(cand, n, q_lim, seed + attempts, quick=True)
                    t_lim = make_limit(time.time(), start_stop, 5, 60)
                    if t_lim > time.time():
                        h2, c2 = try_transforms(h2, c2, n, t_lim)

                    if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_h, best_c = h2, c2
                        if time.time() < start_stop - 90.0:
                            m_lim = make_limit(time.time(), start_stop, 130, 90)
                            if m_lim > time.time() + 15.0:
                                h3, c3 = refine_local(best_h, n, m_lim, seed + 1000 + attempts, quick=False)
                                t_lim2 = make_limit(time.time(), start_stop, 5, 80)
                                if t_lim2 > time.time():
                                    h3, c3 = try_transforms(h3, c3, n, t_lim2)
                                if c3 < best_c - 1e-12:
                                    best_h, best_c = h3, c3

    if (time.time() < stop - 200.0) and (n <= 120):
        n2 = int(n) * 2
        if n2 % 2:
            n2 += 1
        if (2 < n2 <= 400) and (n2 != n):
            h2 = interpolate_to(best_h, n, n2)
            c2 = c5_value(h2, n2)
            if c2 <= best_c + 0.02:
                lim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 180, 150)
                if lim > time.time() + 15.0:
                    h3, c3 = refine_local(h2, n2, lim, seed + 23, quick=True)
                    t_lim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 5, 100)
                    if t_lim > time.time():
                        h3, c3 = try_transforms(h3, c3, n2, t_lim)
                    if c3 < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_h, best_c = h3, c3
                        n = n2

    lim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 80, 25)
    if (lim > time.time() + 10.0) and (n <= 160):
        if n <= 120:
            h2, c2 = active_nlp_refine(best_h, n, lim)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2

        lim2 = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 25, 10)
        if lim2 > time.time() + 5.0:
            h2, c2 = refine_local(best_h, n, lim2, seed + 31, quick=True)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2

    lim = make_limit(time.time(), stop, 20, 5)
    if lim > time.time():
        best_h, best_c = try_transforms(best_h, best_c, n, lim)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_h = force_exact_sum(best_h, n)
    best_c = c5_value(best_h, n)

    for eps in (1e-7, 1e-5):
        h2 = snap_feasible(best_h, eps)
        c2 = c5_value(h2, n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_h = force_exact_sum(best_h, n)
    best_c = c5_value(best_h, n)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(n)
```
